The question becomes retrospectively self-implicating because what emerges and becomes visible concerns human action."}},{"model_id":"delta_earth_speech_handoff","status":"new","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["ح د ث","خ ب ر"],"mechanism":"The human's question is followed by the earth telling her reports. The object spoken about becomes a reporting subject, reversing the initial distribution of voice and knowledge.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"99:3","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001272","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies the human's overt utterance, establishing the first speaker in the exchange."},{"source_ref":"99:4","root":"ح د ث","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000299","branch_id":"B003","role":"The branch supplies speech renewed as report, transferring active narration to the earth."},{"source_ref":"99:4","root":"خ ب ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000387","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies knowledge of a report and an affair's interior, making the earth's response disclosure from informed depth."}],"structural_cues":["The adjacent sequence is human says in 99:3, then she tells her reports in 99:4; the same feminine referent moves from grammatical object of concern to speaking subject."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies adjacent human speech and earth-reporting; I infer a question-to-testimony handoff and a reversal of speaker roles. The alternative is two independent utterances rather than a responsive exchange.","changed_reading":{"before":"The human comments on a mute earth.","after":"The human opens a speech exchange in which the questioned earth takes the floor as an informed witness."}},{"model_id":"delta_answered_laha","status":"revised","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["ر ب ب","و ح ي"],"mechanism":"The open question مَا لَهَا is answered by the reprise أَوْحَىٰ لَهَا: her altered behavior is not autonomous malfunction but response to a sovereign, hidden communication.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"99:3","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001272","branch_id":"B011","role":"The branch supplies interrogative supposition, leaving the cause of the earth's behavior open for the subsequent clause."},{"source_ref":"99:5","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies lordship, ownership, and sovereignty, locating authority behind the earth's action."},{"source_ref":"99:5","root":"و ح ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001633","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies knowledge imparted in hidden fashion, furnishing the causal input that reorganizes the earth's behavior."}],"structural_cues":["The exact prepositional form لَهَا recurs in 99:3 and 99:5, first inside the question and then as the recipient phrase of أَوْحَىٰ."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies the repeated لَهَا, sovereign lordship, and hidden imparting; I read 99:5 as the causal answer to 99:3. The repetition could instead be looser cohesion, but the intervening report clause supports the answer relation.","changed_reading":{"before":"The human confronts an unexplained breakdown in the earth.","after":"The human unknowingly asks for the cause that the sequence names: the earth is carrying out communication received under divine sovereignty."}},{"model_id":"delta_collective_individuation","status":"new","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ن و س","ش ت ت","ر ء ي","ع م ل","ذ ر ر"],"mechanism":"The generic singular human voices one shared question; the later plural humanity disperses and is shown acts at irreducibly small scale. Collective bewilderment resolves into differentiated exposure.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"99:3","root":"ء ن س","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies human presence as either one person or a group, allowing the singular figure to condense humanity's common voice."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ن و س","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"The same human-presence branch now supplies the explicit plural population that unfolds from the focus singular."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ش ت ت","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000775","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies dispersal, breaking the common crowd into differentiated trajectories."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ر ء ي","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000531","branch_id":"B012","role":"The branch supplies being made to see, giving each dispersed human an encounter with displayed evidence."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ع م ل","source_word_indices":["6"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001046","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies intentional acts, the differentiating content returned to the dispersed people."},{"source_ref":"99:7","root":"ذ ر ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000511","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies minute particles, making differentiation persist even at the smallest stated scale."}],"structural_cues":["The sequence shifts from definite generic singular ٱلْإِنسَانُ to plural ٱلنَّاسُ and then repeats singular مَن for the two individually qualified outcomes."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies singular human, plural people, dispersal, shown acts, and particle scale; I infer a movement from one collective cry to individualized disclosure. The live alternative treats the number shifts as ordinary generic variation without a staged social mechanism.","changed_reading":{"before":"One human voices private bewilderment.","after":"The singular question condenses humanity's common shock just before the collective is separated into persons confronted with their own smallest acts."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"outlier_qawl_as_aftershock","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it activates the non-dominant q-w-l to q-l-l mapping rather than the ordinary speech root. It remains anchored in the focus verb and an explicitly packet-authorized split branch, while the preceding quake supplies a matching motion; downstream prose should present it only as a form-and-branch resonance, not as the lexical meaning of قَالَ.","focus_anchor":"The focus verb قَالَ is cited through its packet-supplied non-dominant mapped root whose branch image is a shuddering tremor.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"99:3","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001251","branch_id":"B005","role":"The split branch supplies a shuddering tremor, coloring the focus utterance as speech physically shaken out of the speaker."},{"source_ref":"99:1","root":"ز ل ز ل","source_word_indices":["2","4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000638","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies the actual disturbance and shaking that lets the later vocal resonance function as an aftershock."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"The quake happens, and afterward the human calmly formulates a question.","after":"At the level of contained sound-and-branch resonance, the human utterance is itself an aftershock: the shaken world produces shaken speech."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_pupil_mirror","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it uses the eye's 'little human image' branch of ٱلْإِنسَانُ as an optical metaphor. It remains anchored in the focus noun and is activated by the later showing and viewing of human acts; downstream prose should mark it as a gaze-reversal image, not a lexical replacement for 'human.'","focus_anchor":"The noun ٱلْإِنسَانُ carries a branch image of the human figure reflected in the pupil.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"99:3","root":"ء ن س","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B005","role":"The branch supplies the small human image in the pupil, turning the focus observer into a possible reflected object of vision."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ر ء ي","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000531","branch_id":"B006","role":"The branch supplies view, appearance, and mirror, providing the optical field in which the human can be returned as an image."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ر ء ي","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000531","branch_id":"B012","role":"The branch supplies making visible, reversing the focus human from questioning viewer into one to whom evidence is displayed."},{"source_ref":"99:6","root":"ع م ل","source_word_indices":["6"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001046","branch_id":"B001","role":"The branch supplies intentional acts as the content reflected back into the human's field of vision."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"The human looks outward at an altered earth and asks about it.","after":"The gaze reverses: the outward-looking human becomes figuratively the image in the field of vision as personal acts are displayed back to the observer."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The shaken earth converts the focus question from generic surprise into a breach of the human's familiar ground.","The adjacent move from human speech to the earth's reports makes 99:3 the opening of a witness handoff rather than a comment on a mute object.","The reprise from مَا لَهَا to أَوْحَىٰ لَهَا supplies a causal answer: the earth's altered behavior responds to hidden communication under divine sovereignty.","The later showing of minute acts makes the question retrospectively self-implicating within a disclosure and accountability sequence."],"what_was_special":"The focus question became a hinge among material disturbance, communicative reversal, and individualized exposure. The packet's split mapping also preserved a contained resonance in which shaken earth produces shaken speech.","remaining_uncertainties":["The earth's burdens may be linked to later displayed deeds within one disclosure architecture, or they may remain parallel disclosures.","The repeated لَهَا may form a deliberate question-and-answer link, or may provide looser discourse cohesion.","The q-w-l to q-l-l tremor and pupil-mirror readings are branch-level resonances and should not be promoted to lexical substitutions."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Human speech as bewildered perception, not answer.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B001` uttered speech; `ء ن س B002` perceiving something into awareness by seeing/sensing/hearing. Structural cue: the human speaks only after earth-shaking and earth-output, before earth itself reports. Assigned role: the human’s saying is the first conscious registration of the event, but it has question-form rather than knowledge. Abductive move: I infer that the speech arises because perception has been forced by visible disturbance; the packet gives perception and utterance, I supply the cognitive lag. Reading change: `ما لها` reads less as a rhetorical phrase and more as human awareness catching up to a nonhuman disclosure already underway.\n\n2. **The human as visible presence confronted by a speaking lower world.** Lexical evidence: `ء ن س B001` visible human presence over against hidden/wild beings; activated by `ء ر ض B001` lower ground in 99:1-2 and `ح د ث B003` earth’s speech/report in 99:4. Structural cue: the named human stands between earth’s output and earth’s report. Assigned role: the human is the visible conscious party who assumed the ground was mute, now facing an interlocutor from below. Abductive move: I supply that “earth speaks” reverses the normal human/ground hierarchy: the visible human becomes listener/questioner. Reading change: the ayah reads as social reversal: humanity loses exclusive control of speech.\n\n3. **Question as circulating public talk.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B007` circulating talk/public report; activated by `ن و س B001` people’s swaying mass and `ش ت ت B001` scattered groups in 99:6. Structural cue: a singular `الإنسان` speaks, but later the mass of `الناس` issues in groups. Assigned role: the singular utterance condenses a collective public panic. Abductive move: I infer that the singular human can represent the aggregate whose talk will become many reports because the packet shifts from singular human to people in dispersion. Reading change: `وقال الإنسان` reads as the eruption of shared human discourse, not a lone individual’s sentence.\n\n4. **Inner saying exposed into speech.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B012` unspoken inner saying; `ق و ل B011` saying as supposing; activated by `ر ء ي B002` reflective judgment/opinion in 99:6-8 and `خ ب ر B001` inner knowledge in 99:4. Structural cue: the human’s `ما لها` precedes true news; it is a supposition/question before tested knowledge. Assigned role: speech reveals the interior uncertainty of the human. Abductive move: I supply that question-form discloses an inward judgment gap, because the packet later replaces supposition with seeing. Reading change: the ayah reads as exposure of human interiority before exposure of deeds.","retrospective_surprises_md":"The later `ليروا أعمالهم` corrected my first tendency to treat 99:3 as only panic: it is also a failed or premature epistemic act. Human `قول` asks “what is with her?” before earth’s `أخبار` and before compelled `ر ء ي`; later seeing replaces earlier supposing. The singular `الإنسان` also became more collective after 99:6’s `الناس أشتاتا`.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s099/full_context_control/reader_s099_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **The human is a perceiver whose perception must be corrected.** Fixed `ء ن س/B002` supplies becoming aware by seeing, sensing, or hearing. The question `مَا لَهَا` follows the material disturbance, but 99:6 later uses causative showing (`ر ء ي/B012`) and 99:7-8 use direct seeing (`ر ء ي/B001`). The fixed human noun's role is therefore epistemic: it names the creature trying to bring an unfamiliar event into awareness. The inference is that the failed first perception produces the question and that the later display answers it. `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` thus reads less as a generic species label and more as a perceptual subject placed under correction.\n\n2. **The question marks a rupture of familiarity.** Fixed `ء ن س/B003` supplies familiar comfort that removes estrangement. Structurally, the ordinarily inhabited earth first convulses and expels contents, after which the human asks what is with it; the familiar ground has become an object of inquiry. The dormant familiarity branch assigns the human an affective relation to the earth, and `مَا لَهَا` marks that relation's failure. The assumption that prior earthly stability generated familiarity is inferred. The exclamation then reads as estrangement from what had been the human's most familiar material setting.\n\n3. **Human speech opens a commissioned question-and-testimony exchange.** Fixed `ق و ل/B001` supplies articulated utterance, while `B011` supplies saying with the force of supposition, especially in a question. In the next two ayat the earth gives report-like speech (`ح د ث/B003`) grounded in tested inner knowledge (`خ ب ر/B001`), and that speech is traced to hidden imparting (`و ح ي/B001`). The fixed saying's role is the conjectural first turn; the earth's report supplies the informed response. The inference is that the later report answers this precise question. The ayah consequently reads as the opening of an evidentiary exchange rather than only an uncontrolled cry.","retrospective_surprises_md":"99:4-5 supplied an informed, commissioned answer to the human's conjectural question, turning the apparent monologue into an exchange. More surprisingly, 99:6-8 shift from the human's attempted awareness (`ء ن س/B002`) to being made to see and then directly seeing (`ر ء ي/B012`, `B001`). The repeated singular conditionals in 99:7-8 further correct a species-level reading: the perceptual correction reaches each acting person.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr-11ayah/s099/full_context_control/reader_s099_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_cross_run_publication":{"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-cross-run/tr/99_ayah_findings_publication.json","protocol":"v12-cross-run-publication-v3","language":"tr","surah":99,"ayah_ref":"99:3","canonical_ayah_ref":"99:3","baseline":{"source_ref":"99:3","text":"Ve insan, 'Ona ne oluyor?' dediğinde,","target_tokens":[["Ve",["99:3:1"]],["insan",["99:3:2"]],["Ona",["99:3:4"]],["ne",["99:3:3"]],["oluyor",["99:3:4"]],["dediğinde",["99:3:1"]]]},"findings":[{"text":"İnsanın sorusu, olayı kavramaya çalışan ilk algının başarısızlığını gösterir; sonraki zorunlu gösterim ve doğrudan görme bu algıyı düzeltir.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["99:3:2","root_000059",["B002"]]]},{"text":"Soru, varsayıma dayalı ilk konuşma dönüşüdür; yerin görevlendirilmiş haberi ona bilgiye dayalı cevap verir.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["99:3:1","root_001272",["B001","B011"]]]},{"text":"İnsanın en tanıdık maddi zemini, sarsıntıyla yabancılaşıp hakkında soru sorulan bir şeye dönüşür.","grade":"weak","anchors":[["99:3:2","root_000059",["B003"]]]},{"text":"Görünür insan, konuşma ayrıcalığını yitirip aşağıdaki dünyanın tanıklığını dinleyen tarafa dönüşür.","grade":"weak","anchors":[["99:3:1","root_001272",["B001"]],["99:3:2","root_000059",["B001"]]]},{"text":"Tekil insanın sözü, daha sonra dağılan kalabalığın ortak paniğini ve dolaşıma giren konuşmasını temsil ediyor olabilir.","grade":"reject","anchors":[["99:3:1","root_001272",["B007"]]]},{"text":"İçteki varsayım ve belirsizlik, eylemler açığa çıkmadan önce soru biçiminde dışarı çıkar.","grade":"weak","anchors":[["99:3:1","root_001272",["B011","B012"]]]}]},"butuncul_okuma_line":{"raw_line":"**99:3.** وَقَالَ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ مَا لَهَا — Birincil okuma: İnsan, yerin başına ne geldiğini şaşkınlıkla sorar. Bağlam bu sözü hazır bir cevap değil, görünür sarsıntıya yetişemeyen bir algı ve iç konuşma olarak değiştirir; yer konuşmaya başlayınca insan konuşmanın sahibi olmaktan çıkıp dinleyen ve kendi bilgisizliği açığa çıkan taraf olur. {ق و ل B001; ق و ل B007; ق و ل B011; ق و ل B012; ء ن س B001; ء ن س B002; ح د ث B003; خ ب ر B001}","arabic_text":"وَقَالَ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ مَا لَهَا","reading_text_tr":"İnsan, yerin başına ne geldiğini şaşkınlıkla sorar. Bağlam bu sözü hazır bir cevap değil, görünür sarsıntıya yetişemeyen bir algı ve iç konuşma olarak değiştirir; yer konuşmaya başlayınca insan konuşmanın sahibi olmaktan çıkıp dinleyen ve kendi bilgisizliği açığa çıkan taraf olur.","root_citations":["ق و ل B001","ق و ل B007","ق و ل B011","ق و ل B012","ء ن س B001","ء ن س B002","ح د ث B003","خ ب ر B001"]},"inter_ayah_rows":[{"label":"medium","ref":"96:5","note":"Human knowing and not-knowing supplies a parallel limit on the first response."},{"label":"weak","ref":"33:2","note":"A received-message frame is only a distant parallel to the later explanatory sequence."},{"label":"weak","ref":"4:9","note":"Its regulated speech setting gives only a secondary speech-act parallel."},{"label":"medium","ref":"55:3","note":"Establishes the human addressee, without clarifying the question or its occasion."},{"label":"strong","ref":"99:6","note":"The same scene carries the human question forward to the showing of deeds."},{"label":"medium","ref":"21:4","note":"Extends the contrast between voiced utterance and knowledge beyond what is ## Your response

Write in tr. Write exactly four files at the paths below and modify nothing else. Do not return one combined response. Omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the artifact name.

- Prose: `_commentary/outputs/s099/99_3.prose.tr.md`
- Evidence surface: `_commentary/outputs/s099/99_3.evidence.tr.md`
- Findings index: `_commentary/outputs/s099/99_3.index.tr.md`
- Friction: `_commentary/outputs/s099/99_3.friction.tr.md`

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
